- **ligand-exchange-protocol-manager**: Surface chemistry skill for managing ligand exchange reactions, bioconjugation protocols, and functional group quantification ([a5c-ai/babysitter](https://github.com/a5c-ai/babysitter/tree/HEAD/library/specializations/domains/science/nanotechnology/skills/ligand-exchange-protocol-manager) · ★1.9k)
- **ligand-parameterization**: Detect, plan, and run auditable small-molecule ligand parameterization (GAFF2 via acpype, with sobtop/CGenFF handoffs) that produces the ligand_parameters.json evidence required by the docking-to-MD handoff. ([Frankkk1912/AI4S_Workbench](https://github.com/Frankkk1912/AI4S_Workbench/tree/HEAD/plugins/molecular-modeling-workbench/skills/ligand-parameterization) · 3 copies)
- **ligand-preparation**: Use when ligands need 3D preparation from SMILES or SDF before docking, pocket workflows, receptor-ligand setup, or MD planning. ([ehsansyh/sciagentkit-drug-discovery-skills](https://github.com/ehsansyh/sciagentkit-drug-discovery-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/claude-code-sciagentkit/skills/ligand-preparation) · ★1)
- **ligand-receptor-communication-inference**: Use when you have clustered scRNA-seq data (discrete cell groups or pseudotime-ordered continuous states) and need to predict significant ligand-receptor interactions and signaling pathways between cell populations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/ligand-receptor-communication-inference))
- **ligand-receptor-communication-inference-early-embryo**: Use when analyzing scRNA-seq from developmental systems (e.g. EPI/PE lineages of blastoids or blastocysts) to infer autocrine/paracrine LR signaling dynamics and LR-to-TF causal relationships along pseudotime trajectories. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/ligand-receptor-communication-inference-early-embryo))
- **ligand-receptor-database-curation-validation**: Use when you need to load, audit, or validate a manually curated ligand–receptor interaction database (e.g. ICELLNET Supplementary Data 1) before using it for cell–cell communication scoring, or when reproducing its family-level and subfamily-level interaction counts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/ligand-receptor-database-curation-validation))
- **ligand-receptor-database-mapping**: Use when inferring cell-cell communication from scRNA-seq and interactions must be restricted to literature-supported ligand-receptor pairs, or when comparing tools whose one-ligand/one-receptor assumptions mis-handle multi-subunit receptor complexes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/ligand-receptor-database-mapping))
- **ligand-receptor-database-usage-human-brain**: Use when inferring neural-specific ligand-receptor communication from human single-nucleus/single-cell RNA-seq of brain tissue (e.g., cortical ASD vs control) and you must pick the correct species-specific interaction DB. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/ligand-receptor-database-usage-human-brain))
- **ligand-receptor-downstream-gene-selection**: Use when a signaling pathway's spatial range must be estimated from scRNA-seq data and the canonical ligand, receptor, and downstream target genes for that pathway are known and can be listed explicitly. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/ligand-receptor-downstream-gene-selection))
- **ligand-receptor-edge-quantification**: Use when constructing or reproducing ligand-receptor edge weights from a normalised single-cell expression matrix plus cell-type annotations, e.g. to validate NATMI's ExtractEdges.py weighting scheme or recompute edge_weights.csv independently. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/ligand-receptor-edge-quantification))
- **ligand-receptor-edge-weight-computation**: Use when you need to reproduce, verify, or interpret the edge attributes Connectome emits for every celltype-to-celltype ligand–receptor mechanism, or when you must compute normalized-slot (w1) vs z-score-based (w2) weights independently from Seurat-normalized data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/ligand-receptor-edge-weight-computation))
- **ligand-receptor-expression-filtering**: Use when building ligand–receptor communication edges from single-cell expression data and you need to decide which genes count as 'expressed' per cell type before edge extraction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/ligand-receptor-expression-filtering))
- **ligand-receptor-gene-normalization**: Use when preparing a gene-by-cell-population expression matrix (microarray, bulk RNA-seq, or scRNA-seq) for ICELLNET communication scoring, so that highly expressed molecules like chemokines do not dominate interaction scores. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/ligand-receptor-gene-normalization))
- **ligand-receptor-gene-pair-background-definition**: Use when validating that an identified set of ligand-receptor genes (e.g. eLRs) is enriched or depleted for reference gene sets, and you need a defensible background universe — all protein-coding genes and/or an all-known-LR baseline. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/ligand-receptor-gene-pair-background-definition))
- **ligand-receptor-gene-subsetting-against-fantom5**: Use when preparing scRNA-seq data for Connectome ligand-receptor mapping: the expression matrix must be subset to the union of ligand and receptor genes from ncomms8866_mouse/human before NormalizeData/ScaleData and CreateConnectome. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/ligand-receptor-gene-subsetting-against-fantom5))
- **ligand-receptor-gene-time-series-profiling**: Use when you have expression values for retained L-R pairs across ordered developmental time points (e.g., GSE153164 E12.5–E17.5 corticogenesis) and need to identify which interactions peak at which stage. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/ligand-receptor-gene-time-series-profiling))
- **ligand-receptor-interaction-curation**: Use when building or updating a curated ligand-receptor pair list from existing resources (CellPhoneDB, RNA-Magnet, SingleCellSignalR/LRdb, ICELLNET) and prior connectomes, requiring PubMedID-level literature verification before network inference. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/ligand-receptor-interaction-curation))
- **ligand-receptor-interaction-detection-in-spatial-transcriptomics**: Use when seq-based ST data (e.g. 10x Visium) have been resolved to single-cell resolution via SpatialScope decomposition and you need to identify significant ligand-receptor pairs between spatially co-located cell types, such as EC–SMC signaling in a vascular region of interest. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/ligand-receptor-interaction-detection-in-spatial-transcriptomics))
- **ligand-receptor-interaction-enrichment-analysis**: Use when you have multiple ligand-receptor interaction resources and want to detect over-/underrepresentation of pathway, cancer, or tissue-enriched protein annotations in each resource relative to a pooled background. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/ligand-receptor-interaction-enrichment-analysis))
- **ligand-receptor-interaction-functional-annotation**: Use when you have pre-computed ligand-receptor CCI data (e.g. from CellChat on scRNA-seq) subset to a specific viewpoint such as directed outgoing interactions of one cell cluster, and you need to annotate enriched interaction pairs by biological function before grouping them into modules. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/ligand-receptor-interaction-functional-annotation))
- **ligand-receptor-interaction-inference**: Use when the goal is to infer cell-cell communication from normalized single-cell RNA-seq expression matrices plus cell labels, especially to identify pathway-mediated LR interactions (e.g., CXCL12/CXCR4 between CD8+ T cells and endothelial cells/fibroblasts in HCC). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/ligand-receptor-interaction-inference--9189210baef43d78))
- **ligand-receptor-interaction-inference**: Use when a developmental-series scRNA-seq dataset (expression matrix + cell-type labels + time-point annotations) needs cell-cell interaction inference that distinguishes membrane- from secreted-ligand signaling and tracks dynamic interaction changes across time points. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/ligand-receptor-interaction-inference--7389e460c9b87b8b))
- **ligand-receptor-interaction-inference**: Use when analyzing cell-cell communication in SC data lacking native spatial coordinates but with a matched ST reference (e.g., Stereo-seq E9.5), to compare gene-expression-only vs spatially-informed LRI detection. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/ligand-receptor-interaction-inference--7e7c3f9294df1f8c))
- **ligand-receptor-interaction-pathway-filtering**: Use when scRNA-seq data (e.g., library-size normalized, log-transformed gene-by-cell matrix plus cell labels) has candidate LR pairs and you must prioritize LR interactions by downstream pathway activation in receiver clusters. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/ligand-receptor-interaction-pathway-filtering))
- **ligand-receptor-interaction-prediction-in-disease-skin**: Use when comparing cell-cell communication between two disease states (e.g., lesional vs. nonlesional skin scRNA-seq) to identify significantly upregulated ligand-receptor interactions and shifted information flow. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/ligand-receptor-interaction-prediction-in-disease-skin))
- **ligand-receptor-interaction-rank-aggregation**: Use when multiple CCC method outputs for the same scRNA-seq dataset must be combined into a single consensus prioritisation of ligand-receptor interactions, especially when methods yield incomparable score scales. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/ligand-receptor-interaction-rank-aggregation))
- **ligand-receptor-interaction-scoring**: Use when labeled ground-truth CCI interactions are needed to supervise training of an interaction-inference model (e.g., DeepCCI's Interaction_inference.py), i.e., a scRNA-seq matrix with benchmark cell labels (e.g., panc8, Human) plus a ligand–receptor interaction database. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/ligand-receptor-interaction-scoring--5ca6bfd4326f436e))
- **ligand-receptor-interaction-scoring**: Use when testing whether cell-cell communication strength of candidate eLR pairs depends on a developmental perturbation, such as ZGA inhibition with alpha-amanitin across early/late 2-cell stages. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/ligand-receptor-interaction-scoring--7a986a4847a2ee26))
- **ligand-receptor-interaction-scoring**: Use when a Seurat-processed scRNA-seq dataset has annotated cell clusters and the goal is to identify and rank ligands expressed by one cluster against receptors on another, filtering by &gt;10% expression of ligands/receptors in the clusters of interest. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/ligand-receptor-interaction-scoring--9f7b265c375623bd))
- **ligand-receptor-interaction-scoring**: Use when single-nucleus or single-cell RNA-seq data with cell-type annotations and a curated ligand-receptor pair list (e.g. Human-2020-Jin-LR-pairs.csv) must be converted into per-context communication scores for tensor decomposition. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/ligand-receptor-interaction-scoring--065af9c539507cb1))
- **ligand-receptor-network-inference**: Use when you have a single-cell expression matrix plus cell-type annotations and need to predict which ligand-receptor pairs mediate communication between cell types, including autocrine/self-signalling and network visualisation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/ligand-receptor-network-inference))
- **ligand-receptor-network-mapping**: Use when you have a heterogeneous single-cell RNA-seq dataset with cell-type annotations and want to infer cell–cell communication as ligand–receptor edges (e.g., VEGF, PDGF, DLL3-NOTCH signaling) between cell identities. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/ligand-receptor-network-mapping))
- **ligand-receptor-pair-classification**: Use when building a cell-cell communication network from scRNA-seq data and you must split a curated L-R pair database into membrane-ligand, secreted-ligand, and membrane-receptor classes before computing interaction weights. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/ligand-receptor-pair-classification))
- **ligand-receptor-pair-database-curation**: Use when building or rebuilding the reference LR interaction database needed for cell-cell communication inference (e.g., CommPath, CellPhoneDB-style pipelines) from CellPhoneDB and FANTOM5 resources. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/ligand-receptor-pair-database-curation))
- **ligand-receptor-pair-enrichment**: Use when you have an scRNA-seq count matrix plus cell-type annotations and need to infer, rank, or visualize ligand-receptor-mediated cell-cell communication between annotated cell populations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/ligand-receptor-pair-enrichment))
- **ligand-receptor-pair-filtering**: Use when building a multi-method or multi-context cell-cell communication tensor and you must restrict inputs to LR pairs and cell types shared across all samples, e.g. the 172 shared LR pairs common to all five scoring methods on the BALF COVID-19 data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/ligand-receptor-pair-filtering))
- **ligand-receptor-pair-graph-construction**: Use when scRNA-seq data (CSV or h5) with cell-type labels must be converted into a ligand-receptor pair graph so a GCN model (e.g. DeepCCI's ResNet+GCN interaction model) can classify significant CCIs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/ligand-receptor-pair-graph-construction))
- **ligand-receptor-pair-mapping**: Use when MDIC3 has produced celltype_communication.txt / cellular_communication.txt outputs and you need the specific L-R pairs (e.g., Cx3cl1-Cx3cr1, Apoe-Lrp1, CCL2-CCR2, SST-SSTR2) supporting each sender-receiver communication. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/ligand-receptor-pair-mapping))
- **ligand-receptor-pair-prediction**: Use when you have cluster-annotated scRNA-seq data (human or mouse) and need to infer candidate protein pairs mediating communication between two specific cell types, e.g. CAF1–myeloid interactions in melanoma. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/ligand-receptor-pair-prediction))
- **ligand-receptor-pair-ranking**: Use when you have factor loadings for ligand-receptor pairs from a Tensor-cell2cell decomposition and need to rank them (descending by loading value) to identify top communication pairs or feed factor-specific pre-ranked lists into GSEA. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/ligand-receptor-pair-ranking))
- **ligand-receptor-pair-screening**: Use when analyzing time-course scRNA-seq with a reconstructed trajectory (e.g. monocle3 pseudotime) and a ligand-receptor database to identify which LR pairs co-vary along development. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/ligand-receptor-pair-screening))
- **ligand-receptor-perturbation-scoring**: Use when comparing two Seurat/Connectome connectomes (e.g., IFN-stimulated vs control PBMCs) and you need a sign-agnostic per-edge magnitude of perturbation from ligand.norm.lfc and recept.norm.lfc values. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/ligand-receptor-perturbation-scoring))
- **ligand-receptor-resource-loading**: Use when an analysis needs to enumerate, compare, or overlap multiple CCC ligand-receptor resources (e.g., CellChatDB, CellPhoneDB, Ramilowski, ConnectomeDB, LRdb) before downstream inference or benchmarking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/ligand-receptor-resource-loading))
- **ligand-target-communication-strength-computation**: Use when reproducing or auditing NeuronChat's ligand/target abundance and communication-strength computation for small-molecule neurotransmitter signaling from single-cell expression data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/ligand-target-communication-strength-computation))
- **ligandmpnn**: Inverse-fold a backbone with ligand, nucleic-acid, and metal context using LigandMPNN (Dauparas et al. 2023, github.com/dauparas/LigandMPNN). Reach for this skill to redesign the residues lining a binding pocket around a bound small molecule or cofactor, to design metal-coordinating sites where the geometry must be respected, or to get threaded designed-sequence PDBs out of any MPNN run. ([aipoch/open-science](https://github.com/aipoch/open-science/tree/HEAD/resources/skills/ligandmpnn) · ★5.5k · 20 copies)
- **ligandmpnn**: Ligand-aware protein sequence design using LigandMPNN. Use this skill when: (1) Designing sequences around small molecules, (2) Enzyme active site design, (3) Ligand binding pocket optimization, (4) Metal coordination site design, (5) Cofactor binding proteins. For standard protein design, use proteinmpnn. For solubility optimization, use solublempnn. ([FreedomIntelligence/OpenClaw-Medical-Skills](https://github.com/FreedomIntelligence/OpenClaw-Medical-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/ligandmpnn) · ★3.1k · 21 copies)
- **ligandmpnn**: This page used to hold an archived copy of this skill. The archive now lives in the registry's data repository. ([majiayu000/claude-skill-registry](https://github.com/majiayu000/claude-skill-registry/tree/HEAD/skills/analysis/ligandmpnn) · ★745)
- **ligandmpnn**: Extends ProteinMPNN to design sequences around small molecules, metal ions, nucleic acids, and other non-protein entities. Essential for enzyme active site design and ligand-binding protein engineering. ([lamm-mit/scienceclaw](https://github.com/lamm-mit/scienceclaw/tree/HEAD/skills/ligandmpnn) · ★251)
- **ligandmpnn**: ligandmpnn (Ligand Message Passing Neural Network) is a deep learning model for atomic context-conditioned protein sequence design. It incorporates ligand and other non-protein atoms (such as DNA, RNA, or small molecules) as context for protein sequence design. Use this model when you need to design novel amino acid sequences for protein backbones with ligand awareness. ([mindspore-ai/mindscience](https://github.com/mindspore-ai/mindscience/tree/HEAD/MindScienceSkills/biology/protein_and_structure/ligandmpnn) · ★56)
- **ligandmpnn**: Design protein sequences around ligand or small-molecule contexts with LigandMPNN-style workflows. Use when a task asks for ligand-aware protein design, residue redesign, constraints, or design ranking. ([Yichen-ZJU/lemvo](https://github.com/Yichen-ZJU/lemvo/tree/HEAD/claude/skills/ligandmpnn) · ★12 · 4 copies)
- **ligandmpnn**: LigandMPNN inverse folding: designs protein sequences onto a fixed backbone while conditioning on neighboring non-protein atoms, such as redesigning an antibody around a bound antigen, a pocket around a ligand, or a protein around DNA or metals. Reach for it when sequence design on an existing structure is needed; it returns designed sequences with per-design confidence and sequence-recovery numbers, not folded structures. ([brineylab/toolshed](https://github.com/brineylab/toolshed/tree/HEAD/tools/ligandmpnn))
- **ligandmpnn-adaptyvbio-protein-design-skill**: This page used to hold an archived copy of this skill. The archive now lives in the registry's data repository. ([majiayu000/claude-skill-registry](https://github.com/majiayu000/claude-skill-registry/tree/HEAD/skills/analysis/ligandmpnn-adaptyvbio-protein-design-skill) · ★745)
- **ligandmpnn-adaptyvbio-protein-design-skill-2**: This page used to hold an archived copy of this skill. The archive now lives in the registry's data repository. - **ligandmpnn-adaptyvbio-protein-design-skill-3**: This page used to hold an archived copy of this skill. The archive now lives in the registry's data repository. - **ligandmpnn-runner**: 在当前项目中使用 LigandMPNN 做蛋白序列设计，或用户提到“跑 ligandmpnn”“做序列设计”“根据复合物设计序列”“给 pdb 生成序列”时务必使用。指导 agent 选择必要参数、严格通过 submit_job 异步提交到 ligandmpnn-mcp，并依据本地 run 包装层、Docker 配置和结果目录语义正确汇报。 ([helplessness/benchthread](https://github.com/helplessness/benchthread/tree/HEAD/.tcode/skills/ligandmpnn-runner) · ★4)
- **ligandmpnn_skill**: Inverse-fold a backbone with ligand, nucleic-acid, and metal context using LigandMPNN (Dauparas et al. 2023, github.com/dauparas/LigandMPNN). Reach for this skill to redesign the residues lining a binding pocket around a bound small molecule or cofactor, to design metal-coordinating sites where the geometry must be respected, or to get threaded designed-sequence PDBs out of any MPNN run. ([DVampire/Autogenesis](https://github.com/DVampire/Autogenesis/tree/HEAD/autogenesis/skill/science/ligandmpnn_skill) · ★71)
- **ligar-ao-codex**: Liga o Claude Code ao app do Codex (o "ChatGPT" para Windows) que está aberto na tela. Delega tarefas a um chat do Codex com o modelo e o esforço que o usuário escolher, espera o Codex avisar que terminou e revisa o que ele fez. Também lista, cria, abre, lê, renomeia, arquiva e apaga chats do Codex, e tudo aparece na hora no app. Usar quando o usuário disser "ligar ao Codex", "manda/delega/passa pro Codex", "pede pro Codex fazer", "o Codex faz", "chat do Codex" (criar, abrir, renomear, arquivar, excluir, listar), "modelo/esforço do Codex" ou "uso/limite do Codex". ([LucasDias777/skills-e-mcps](https://github.com/LucasDias777/skills-e-mcps/tree/HEAD/skills/ligar-ao-codex))
- **liger-kernel**: Decide where and how to wire Liger Kernel into an MVP-Engine recipe using LigerKernelKit, including official family dispatch, authoring a custom or composite model's LigerPatch map, module selection, and loss-kernel safety. ([mvp-ai-lab/mvp-engine](https://github.com/mvp-ai-lab/mvp-engine/tree/HEAD/skills/model/liger-kernel) · ★25)
- **liger-kernel-kit**: Use LigerKernelKit for reusable Liger Kernel integration before model construction, covering official model-family dispatch, custom-model symbol patching via LigerPatch, module selection validation, and loss-kernel guards. ([mvp-ai-lab/mvp-engine](https://github.com/mvp-ai-lab/mvp-engine/tree/HEAD/skills/kit/liger-kernel-kit) · ★25)
- **light**: 3-layer lighting plan per zone — ambient, task, and accent with numeric lumen targets, one color temperature from the concept, pendant geometry per LIGHT-01, and outlet checks.
